  qds.signature.generate_signature                   | Time: O(n)       | Mem: O(n)

  security.fingerprint.build_fingerprint             | Time: O(n)       | Mem: O(b)
  security.thresholds.percentile_calibrate           | Time: O(m log m) | Mem: O(m)
  security.anomaly.compute_anomaly_breakdown         | Time: O(b)       | Mem: O(b)
  attacks.replay.ReplayAttack                        | Time: O(1)       | Mem: O(1)

=> Benchmark and computational results stored in 'experiments\results'
